- **polars_horizontal**: Horizontal calculation across multiple columns. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_horizontal.md) · ★12)
- **polars_implode**: Aggregates values into a list. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_implode.md) · ★12)
- **polars_integer**: Converts a string column into a integer column ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_integer.md) · ★12)
- **polars_into-df**: Converts a list, table or record into a dataframe. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_into-df.md) · ★12)
- **polars_into-dtype**: Convert a string to a specific datatype. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_into-dtype.md) · ★12)
- **polars_into-lazy**: Converts a dataframe into a lazy dataframe. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_into-lazy.md) · ★12)
- **polars_into-nu**: Converts a dataframe or an expression into nushell value for access and exploration. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_into-nu.md) · ★12)
- **polars_into-repr**: Display a dataframe in its repr format. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_into-repr.md) · ★12)
- **polars_into-schema**: Convert a value to a polars schema object ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_into-schema.md) · ★12)
- **polars_is-duplicated**: Creates mask indicating duplicated values. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_is-duplicated.md) · ★12)
- **polars_is-in**: Creates an is-in expression or checks to see if the elements are contained in the right series ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_is-in.md) · ★12)
- **polars_is-not-null**: Creates mask where value is not null. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_is-not-null.md) · ★12)
- **polars_is-null**: Creates mask where value is null. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_is-null.md) · ★12)
- **polars_is-unique**: Creates mask indicating unique values. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_is-unique.md) · ★12)
- **polars_join**: Joins a lazy frame with other lazy frame. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_join.md) · ★12)
- **polars_join-where**: Joins a lazy frame with other lazy frame based on conditions. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_join-where.md) · ★12)
- **polars_last**: Creates new dataframe with tail rows or creates a last expression. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_last.md) · ★12)
- **polars_len**: Return the number of rows in the context. This is similar to COUNT(*) in SQL. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_len.md) · ★12)
- **polars_list-contains**: Checks if an element is contained in a list. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_list-contains.md) · ★12)
- **polars_lit**: Creates a literal expression. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_lit.md) · ★12)
- **polars_lowercase**: Lowercase the strings in the column. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_lowercase.md) · ★12)
- **polars_map-batches**: Map a custom Nushell closure over one or more dataframe columns. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_map-batches.md) · ★12)
- **polars_math**: Collection of math functions to be applied on column expressions. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_math.md) · ★12)
- **polars_math_abs**: Compute the absolute values of a column expression. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_math_abs.md) · ★12)
- **polars_math_bitwise-and**: Perform an aggregation of bitwise ANDs over a column expression. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_math_bitwise-and.md) · ★12)
- **polars_math_bitwise-count-ones**: Compute the number of set bits for each element in an integer column expression. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_math_bitwise-count-ones.md) · ★12)
- **polars_math_bitwise-count-zeros**: Compute the number of unset bits for each element in an integer column expression. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_math_bitwise-count-zeros.md) · ★12)
- **polars_math_bitwise-leading-ones**: Compute the number of leading set bits for each element in an integer column expression. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_math_bitwise-leading-ones.md) · ★12)
- **polars_math_bitwise-leading-zeros**: Compute the number of leading unset bits for each element in an integer column expression. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_math_bitwise-leading-zeros.md) · ★12)
- **polars_math_bitwise-or**: Perform an aggregation of bitwise ORs over a column expression. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_math_bitwise-or.md) · ★12)
- **polars_math_bitwise-trailing-ones**: Compute the number of trailing set bits for each element in an integer column expression. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_math_bitwise-trailing-ones.md) · ★12)
- **polars_math_bitwise-trailing-zeros**: Compute the number of trailing unset bits for each element in an integer column expression. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_math_bitwise-trailing-zeros.md) · ★12)
- **polars_math_bitwise-xor**: Perform an aggregation of bitwise XORs over a column expression. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_math_bitwise-xor.md) · ★12)
- **polars_math_cos**: Compute the element-wise cosine of a column expression. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_math_cos.md) · ★12)
- **polars_math_dot**: Compute the dot product of two column expressions. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_math_dot.md) · ★12)
- **polars_math_exp**: Compute element-wise e raised to the power of a column expression. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_math_exp.md) · ★12)
- **polars_math_log**: Compute the element-wise logarithm of a column expression. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_math_log.md) · ★12)
- **polars_math_log1p**: Compute the element-wise natural log of 1 + x for a column expression. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_math_log1p.md) · ★12)
- **polars_math_sign**: Compute the element-wise sign of a column expression, returning -1, 0, or 1. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_math_sign.md) · ★12)
- **polars_math_sin**: Compute the element-wise sine of a column expression. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_math_sin.md) · ★12)
- **polars_math_sqrt**: Compute the element-wise square root of a column expression. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_math_sqrt.md) · ★12)
- **polars_max**: Creates a max expression or aggregates columns to their max value. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_max.md) · ★12)
- **polars_mean**: Creates a mean expression for an aggregation or aggregates columns to their mean value. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_mean.md) · ★12)
- **polars_median**: Median value from columns in a dataframe or creates expression for an aggregation ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_median.md) · ★12)
- **polars_min**: Creates a min expression or aggregates columns to their min value. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_min.md) · ★12)
- **polars_n-unique**: Counts unique values. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_n-unique.md) · ★12)
- **polars_not**: Inverts boolean mask. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_not.md) · ★12)
- **polars_open**: Opens CSV, JSON, NDJSON/JSON lines, arrow, avro, or parquet file to create dataframe. A lazy dataframe will be created by default, if supported. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_open.md) · ★12)
- **polars_otherwise**: Completes a when expression. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_otherwise.md) · ★12)
- **polars_over**: Compute expressions over a window group defined by partition expressions. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_over.md) · ★12)
- **polars_pivot**: Pivot a DataFrame from long to wide format. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_pivot.md) · ★12)
- **polars_profile**: Profile a lazy dataframe. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_profile.md) · ★12)
- **polars_qcut**: Bin continuous values into discrete categories based on their quantiles for a series. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_qcut.md) · ★12)
- **polars_quantile**: Aggregates the columns to the selected quantile. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_quantile.md) · ★12)
- **polars_query**: Query dataframe using SQL. Note: The dataframe is always named 'df' in your query's from clause. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_query.md) · ★12)
- **polars_rename**: Rename a dataframe column. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_rename.md) · ★12)
- **polars_replace**: Create an expression that replaces old values with new values ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_replace.md) · ★12)
- **polars_replace-time-zone**: Replace the timezone information in a datetime column. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_replace-time-zone.md) · ★12)
- **polars_reverse**: Reverses the LazyFrame ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_reverse.md) · ★12)
- **polars_rolling**: Rolling calculation for a series or expression, or a rolling group-by for a lazyframe. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_rolling.md) · ★12)
- **polars_sample**: Create sample dataframe. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_sample.md) · ★12)
- **polars_save**: Saves a dataframe to disk. For lazy dataframes a sink operation will be used if the file type supports it (parquet, ipc/arrow, csv, and ndjson). ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_save.md) · ★12)
- **polars_schema**: Show schema for a dataframe. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_schema.md) · ★12)
- **polars_select**: Selects columns from lazyframe. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_select.md) · ★12)
- **polars_selector**: Create column selectors for use in polars commands. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_selector.md) · ★12)
- **polars_selector_all**: Creates a selector that selects all columns. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_selector_all.md) · ★12)
- **polars_selector_alpha**: Select all columns with alphabetic names (eg: only letters). Matching column names cannot contain *any* non-alphabetic characters. Note that the definition of \"alphabetic\" consi... ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_selector_alpha.md) · ★12)
- **polars_selector_alphanumeric**: Select all columns with alphanumeric names (eg: only letters). Matching column names cannot contain *any* non-alphanumeric characters. Note that the definition of \"alphanumeric\"... ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_selector_alphanumeric.md) · ★12)
- **polars_selector_array**: Select all array columns. Optionally filter by fixed width. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_selector_array.md) · ★12)
- **polars_selector_binary**: Select all binary columns. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_selector_binary.md) · ★12)
- **polars_selector_boolean**: Select all boolean columns. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_selector_boolean.md) · ★12)
- **polars_selector_by-dtype**: Creates a selector that selects columns by data type. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_selector_by-dtype.md) · ★12)
- **polars_selector_by-index**: Select columns by their index position. Supports negative indices (e.g., -1 for the last column). ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_selector_by-index.md) · ★12)
- **polars_selector_by-name**: Creates a selector that selects columns by name. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_selector_by-name.md) · ★12)
- **polars_selector_categorical**: Select all categorical columns. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_selector_categorical.md) · ★12)
- **polars_selector_contains**: Select columns whose names contain the given literal substring(s). ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_selector_contains.md) · ★12)
- **polars_selector_date**: Select all date columns. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_selector_date.md) · ★12)
- **polars_selector_datetime**: Select all datetime columns. Optionally filter by time unit (ns, us, ms) and/or timezone. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_selector_datetime.md) · ★12)
- **polars_selector_decimal**: Select all decimal columns. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_selector_decimal.md) · ★12)
- **polars_selector_digit**: Select columns whose names consist entirely of digit characters. By default uses Unicode decimal digits; use `--ascii-only` to restrict to ASCII 0-9. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_selector_digit.md) · ★12)
- **polars_selector_duration**: Select all duration columns. Optionally filter by time unit (ns, us, ms). ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_selector_duration.md) · ★12)
- **polars_selector_empty**: Create an empty selector that matches no columns. Useful as a base for selector composition. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_selector_empty.md) · ★12)
- **polars_selector_ends-with**: Select columns that end with the given substring(s). ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_selector_ends-with.md) · ★12)
- **polars_selector_enum**: Select all enum columns. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_selector_enum.md) · ★12)
- **polars_selector_exclude**: Select all columns except those with the given name(s). This is the inverse of `polars selector by-name`. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_selector_exclude.md) · ★12)
- **polars_selector_first**: Creates a selector that selects the first column(s) by index. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_selector_first.md) · ★12)
- **polars_selector_float**: Select all float columns. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_selector_float.md) · ★12)
- **polars_selector_integer**: Select all integer columns. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_selector_integer.md) · ★12)
- **polars_selector_last**: Creates a selector that selects the last column(s) by index. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_selector_last.md) · ★12)
- **polars_selector_list**: Select all list columns. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_selector_list.md) · ★12)
- **polars_selector_matches**: Select all columns that match the given regex pattern. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_selector_matches.md) · ★12)
- **polars_selector_nested**: Select all nested columns (list, array, or struct). ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_selector_nested.md) · ★12)
- **polars_selector_not**: Inverts selector. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_selector_not.md) · ★12)
- **polars_selector_numeric**: Select all numeric columns. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_selector_numeric.md) · ★12)
- **polars_selector_object**: Select all object columns. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_selector_object.md) · ★12)
- **polars_selector_signed-integer**: Select all signed integer columns. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_selector_signed-integer.md) · ★12)
- **polars_selector_starts-with**: Select columns that start with the given substring(s). ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_selector_starts-with.md) · ★12)
- **polars_selector_string**: Select all string columns. Use `--include-categorical` to also select categorical columns. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/polars_selector_string.md) · ★12)

- **portaljs-add-chart**: Add a chart (line, bar, area, pie, or scatter) to a dataset's showcase in a PortalJS portal. Installs recharts, writes a reusable Chart component, and renders it in the showcase Views section. ([datopian/portaljs](https://github.com/datopian/portaljs/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/portaljs-add-chart.md) · ★2.4k)
- **portaljs-add-dataset**: Add a dataset (CSV, TSV, JSON, or GeoJSON) to an existing PortalJS portal. Appends an entry to datasets.json so the catalog and showcase render it automatically; routes the data by source (local file vs remote URL) — R2 via Git LFS by default, remote URLs by passthrough. ([datopian/portaljs](https://github.com/datopian/portaljs/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/portaljs-add-dataset.md) · ★2.4k)
- **portaljs-add-dcat**: Make a PortalJS portal harvestable by national/EU/US open-data portals — emit standards-compliant DCAT catalog feeds (DCAT 2/3, DCAT-AP, DCAT-US, national profiles) in JSON-LD, Turtle, and RDF/XML at build, with autodiscovery and per-profile conformance checking. ([datopian/portaljs](https://github.com/datopian/portaljs/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/portaljs-add-dcat.md) · ★2.4k)
- **portaljs-add-geo**: Auto-ingest a geospatial file (GeoJSON, Shapefile, GeoPackage, KML/KMZ, FlatGeobuf, CSV-with-geometry) into a PortalJS portal — on your own machine, no server. Normalizes CRS to EPSG:4326, derives a PMTiles render tier + a GeoParquet query tier, pushes all three to R2 via Git LFS, and emits one dual-tier datasets.json entry the showcase renders with &lt;MapPreview&gt; + &lt;GeoQuery&gt;. ([datopian/portaljs](https://github.com/datopian/portaljs/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/portaljs-add-geo.md) · ★2.4k)
- **portaljs-add-map**: Render a GeoJSON dataset on an interactive Leaflet map in the Views section of a dataset's showcase. Installs react-leaflet and a Map component, then renders the map for the chosen dataset. ([datopian/portaljs](https://github.com/datopian/portaljs/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/portaljs-add-map.md) · ★2.4k)
- **portaljs-add-resource**: Add another file (resource) to an EXISTING dataset in a PortalJS portal — a data dictionary, methodology, or an additional data file. Turns a single-file dataset into a multi-resource one; the showcase renders a section per resource. ([datopian/portaljs](https://github.com/datopian/portaljs/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/portaljs-add-resource.md) · ★2.4k)
- **portaljs-architect**: Recommend a data-portal architecture (storage, compute, catalog, access, hosting, metadata) from your needs, then hand off to the build skills. The advisory entry point. ([datopian/portaljs](https://github.com/datopian/portaljs/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/portaljs-deploy.md) · ★2.4k)
- **portaljs-migrate**: Migrate (harvest) datasets between open-data platforms. Reads CKAN, a DCAT-US /data.json catalog (DKAN, ArcGIS Hub, data.gov), a DCAT / DCAT-AP RDF feed (JSON-LD, Turtle, or RDF/XML — data.europa.eu, national DCAT-AP portals, GeoDCAT-AP), Socrata, OpenDataSoft, or an ArcGIS FeatureServer, and writes them to a static PortalJS catalog (datasets.json, link-by-URL or download data files into Cloudflare R2 via Git LFS / Giftless) or pushes them into a CKAN instance over its API.
